A request for date `D` uses the latest publication date on or\nbefore `D`. This service is the single place that rule lives; the rates and conversion routes call it.\n\n## Interface\n\nModule `services/effective_date.py` (a top-level `services` package):\n\n`effective_publication(db, requested: str | None) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str]] | None`\n\n- `requested` is an ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` string, or `None` meaning the latest publication.\n- Makes exactly one `db.rates.list_through(...)` call: with `through_date=requested` when a date is given,\n  with no bound when `None`.\n- The rows arrive ordered by date descending then currency ascending. The first row's date is the\n  effective date. The rates are the rows carrying that date, as a mapping of currency code to stored rate text.\n- Returns `None` when no row exists on or before the requested date.\n- Dates are compared as ISO strings. Rate values are the stored text, never converted to a number.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-002` — read: list rates on or before a date - supplies: `through_date` - returns: `date`, `currency`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC effective-date-exact-and-between ===\nIntent: A requested date that is a publication uses itself, and a later requested date uses the latest earlier publication; It uses the\n  request-scoped database dependency from the web foundation.\n- The path date must match `YYYY-MM-DD` and be a real calendar date; otherwise respond 422 through\n  FastAPI request validation.\n- The effective date and rates come from `services.effective_date.effective_publication`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-exact ===\nIntent: A request for a publication date returns that date as both effective and requested, base EUR, and exactly that date's rates as stored text, excluding other dates.\nRequires: executable=python3; it calls the effective-date service.\n- Application startup never initializes the schema.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-RatesLatest.md ===\n# FEATURE: Rates Latest\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Serve GET /rates/latest, returning every currency's rate on the most recent publication date. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-RatesByDate.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /rates/latest |\n| Consumes | rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`GET /rates/latest` returns the rates for the latest publication without the caller knowing its date.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /rates/latest` | 200 with `{\"date\": <latest publication date>, \"requested\": \"latest\", \"base\": \"EUR\", \"rates\": {<code>: <rate>, ...}}`. 404 when the store holds no rates. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Register this route in the shared rates router in `app_server/routers/rates.py` ahead of\n  `GET /rates/{date}`, so `latest` is never parsed as a date and rejected with 422.\n- Resolve through `services.effective_date.effective_publication(db, None)`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC rates-latest-returns-latest ===\nIntent: The latest route returns the most recent publication, with requested set to the latest token, and is not shadowed by the dated route; a far-future seeded date is always the latest.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nlatest = \"9999-12-31\"\nearlier = \"9999-12-30\"\nlatest_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"1.2300\", \"ZZB\": \"45.6\"}\nearlier_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"7\", \"ZZC\": \"8\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in latest_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    for cur, rate in earlier_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=earlier, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\"/rates/latest\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"date\"] == latest\n    assert body[\"requested\"] == \"latest\"\n    assert body[\"base\"] == \"EUR\"\n    assert body[\"rates\"] == latest_rates\n=== END AC rates-latest-returns-latest ===\n\n=== AC rates-latest-matches-dated ===\nIntent: The latest route and the dated route for the latest publication date return the same effective date and rates.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nlatest = \"9999-12-31\"\nrates_in = {\"ZZA\": \"0.9430\", \"ZZB\": \"11.4555\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in rates_in.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    by_latest = client.get(\"/rates/latest\")\n    assert by_latest.status_code == 200\n    by_date = client.get(f\"/rates/{latest}\")\n    assert by_date.status_code == 200\n    a = by_latest.json()\n    b = by_date.json()\n    assert a[\"date\"] == b[\"date\"]\n    assert a[\"rates\"] == b[\"rates\"]\n    assert a[\"rates\"] == rates_in\n=== END AC rates-latest-matches-dated ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `/rates/latest` is never answered with 422 as though `latest` were a malformed date.\n- Rate values are the stored text as JSON strings.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Convert.md ===\n# FEATURE: Convert\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Serve GET /convert for an explicit date, converting an amount between two currencies with exact decimal arithmetic. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-WebFoundation.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /convert |\n| Consumes | rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nConvert an amount from one currency to another using the euro reference rates on the effective date.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /convert?from=<code>&to=<code>&amount=<amount>&date=<date>` | 200 with `{\"date\": <effective date>, \"from\": <code>, \"to\": <code>, \"amount\": <amount>, \"rate\": <rate>, \"result\": <result>}`; every value is a JSON string. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The router lives in `app_server/routers/convert.py`, included by `create_app()`. Parameters are\n  `from` (Python name via alias), `to`, `amount` and `date`.\n- The effective date and rates come from `services.effective_date.effective_publication`.\n- EUR has the implicit rate `1`; any other currency takes its stored rate on the effective date.\n- All arithmetic uses `decimal.Decimal` from the stored text. Never use floats.\n- `rate` = `rate(to) / rate(from)`, rounded half to even to six decimals, written with exactly six decimals.\n- `result` = `amount * rate(to) / rate(from)`, computed from the stored rates and never from the rounded\n  `rate`, rounded half to even to two decimals, written with exactly two decimals.\n- `amount` is echoed exactly as given, as a string, and must match `[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?`.\n- Handling of missing publications, unknown currencies and invalid parameters is specified by\n  FEATURE-ConvertErrors.md; this story implements the successful conversion.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC convert-cross-currency-fallback-date ===\nIntent: A cross conversion on a non-publication date uses the earlier effective date and returns rate and result derived from the stored rates with half-even rounding.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\npublished = \"1800-07-01\"\nrequested = \"1800-07-05\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nfrom_rate, to_rate = \"1.0298\", \"0.84453\"\namount = \"100\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=published, currency=from_cur, rate=from_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=published, currency=to_cur, rate=to_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": requested},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    rf, rt, amt = Decimal(from_rate), Decimal(to_rate), Decimal(amount)\n    rate = (rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (amt * rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    assert body[\"date\"] == published\n    assert body[\"from\"] == from_cur\n    assert body[\"to\"] == to_cur\n    assert body[\"amount\"] == amount\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-cross-currency-fallback-date ===\n\n=== AC convert-eur-legs ===\nIntent: EUR carries the implicit rate 1 as source and as target, and the amount is echoed exactly as given including its decimals.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nday = \"1800-07-10\"\ncur = \"ZZA\"\ncur_rate = \"161.77\"\namount = \"250.25\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=cur, rate=cur_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n\n    out = client.get(\"/convert\", params={\"from\": \"EUR\", \"to\": cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day})\n    assert out.status_code == 200\n    body = out.json()\n    rate = (Decimal(cur_rate) / Decimal(1)).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (Decimal(amount) * Decimal(cur_rate) / Decimal(1)).quantize(\n        Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n    )\n    assert body[\"amount\"] == amount\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n\n    back = client.get(\"/convert\", params={\"from\": cur, \"to\": \"EUR\", \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day})\n    assert back.status_code == 200\n    body = back.json()\n    rate = (Decimal(1) / Decimal(cur_rate)).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (Decimal(amount) * Decimal(1) / Decimal(cur_rate)).quantize(\n        Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n    )\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-eur-legs ===\n\n=== AC convert-result-from-unrounded-rate ===\nIntent: The result is computed from the stored rates, not from the six-decimal rounded rate: with a from rate 3 and a to rate 1 the exact result differs from amount times the rounded rate.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nday = \"1800-07-15\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nfrom_rate, to_rate = \"3\", \"1\"\namount = \"3000000\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=from_cur, rate=from_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=to_cur, rate=to_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    rf, rt, amt = Decimal(from_rate), Decimal(to_rate), Decimal(amount)\n    rounded_rate = (rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    exact = (amt * rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    from_rounded = (amt * rounded_rate).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    assert exact != from_rounded\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rounded_rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{exact:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-result-from-unrounded-rate ===\n\n=== AC convert-half-even-tie ===\nIntent: A result exactly halfway between two cents rounds half to even, so 0.125 at equal rates becomes the even neighbour rather than rounding up.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN, ROUND_HALF_UP\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nday = \"1800-07-20\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nsame_rate = \"2\"\namount = \"0.125\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=from_cur, rate=same_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=to_cur, rate=same_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    exact = Decimal(amount) * Decimal(same_rate) / Decimal(same_rate)\n    even = exact.quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    up = exact.quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)\n    assert even != up\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{even:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-half-even-tie ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never use floating point for rates, amounts or results; use `decimal` on the stored text.\n- `result` is never derived from the rounded `rate`.\n- `amount` is echoed exactly as received, not normalized.\n- The route does not query the database directly; it calls the effective-date service.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ConvertLatest.md ===\n# FEATURE: Convert Latest\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Make the date parameter of GET /convert optional, defaulting to the latest publication. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-Convert.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /convert latest default |\n| Consumes | GET /convert, rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nA caller who omits `date` gets a conversion at the most recent published rates.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /convert?from=<code>&to=<code>&amount=<amount>` | As `GET /convert`, using the latest publication date; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md ===\n# FEATURE: Effective Date\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Service function that resolves the latest publication date on or before a requested date and returns that date's rates. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides | rates.effective_publication |\n| Consumes | db.rates.list_through |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe ECB publishes on working days only. A request for date `D` uses the latest publication date on or\nbefore `D`. This service is the single place that rule lives; the rates and conversion routes call it.\n\n## Interface\n\nModule `services/effective_date.py` (a top-level `services` package):\n\n`effective_publication(db, requested: str | None) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str]] | None`\n\n- `requested` is an ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` string, or `None` meaning the latest publication.\n- Makes exactly one `db.rates.list_through(...)` call: with `through_date=requested` when a date is given,\n  with no bound when `None`.\n- The rows arrive ordered by date descending then currency ascending. The first row's date is the\n  effective date. The rates are the rows carrying that date, as a mapping of currency code to stored rate text.\n- Returns `None` when no row exists on or before the requested date.\n- Dates are compared as ISO strings. Rate values are the stored text, never converted to a number.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-002` — read: list rates on or before a date - supplies: `through_date` - returns: `date`, `currency`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC effective-date-exact-and-between ===\nIntent: A requested date that is a publication uses itself, and a later requested date uses the latest earlier publication; only the effective date's rates are returned as stored text.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom services.effective_date import effective_publication\n\nolder = \"1800-05-08\"\nnewer = \"1800-05-10\"\nlater_request = \"1800-05-12\"\nolder_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"1.1000\", \"ZZB\": \"9.9\"}\nnewer_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"2.2500\", \"ZZB\": \"8.8\", \"ZZC\": \"7\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in older_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=older, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    for cur, rate in newer_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=newer, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n\n    found = effective_publication(db, newer)\n    assert found is not None\n    date, rates = found\n    assert date == newer\n    assert rates == newer_rates\n\n    found = effective_publication(db, later_request)\n    assert found is not None\n    date, rates = found\n    assert date == newer\n    assert rates == newer_rates\n\n    found = effective_publication(db, \"1800-05-09\")\n    assert found is not None\n    date, rates = found\n    assert date == older\n    assert rates == older_rates\n=== END AC effective-date-exact-and-between ===\n\n=== AC effective-date-none-before ===\nIntent: A requested date earlier than every stored publication yields None rather than an error or an empty result.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom services.effective_date import effective_publication\n\nseeded = \"1800-05-20\"\ntoo_early = \"1799-01-01\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=seeded, currency=\"ZZA\", rate=\"1.5\")\n    assert effective_publication(db, too_early) is None\n=== END AC effective-date-none-before ===\n\n=== AC effective-date-latest ===\nIntent: Passing None resolves the latest publication in the store; a far-future seeded date is always the latest regardless of existing data.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom services.effective_date import effective_publication\n\nlatest = \"9999-12-31\"\nrates_in = {\"ZZA\": \"3.1400\", \"ZZB\": \"2.7\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in rates_in.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    found = effective_publication(db, None)\n    assert found is not None\n    date, rates = found\n    assert date == latest\n    assert rates == rates_in\n    for value in rates.values():\n        assert isinstance(value, str)\n=== END AC effective-date-latest ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Rate values stay as stored text; no float or Decimal conversion happens in this service.\n- The function reaches the store only through `db.rates.list_through`.\n- Dates are compared as ISO strings, never via floating point or locale parsing.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-RatesByDate.md ===\n# FEATURE: Rates By Date\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Serve GET /rates/{date}, returning every currency's rate on the effective publication date. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-WebFoundation.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /rates/{date} |\n| Consumes | rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`GET /rates/{date}` answers what the euro reference rates were on a date, falling back to the latest\npublication on or before it.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /rates/{date}` | 200 with `{\"date\": <effective date>, \"requested\": <date>, \"base\": \"EUR\", \"rates\": {<code>: <rate>, ...}}`. Rates are the stored text as JSON strings. 404 when no publication is on or before the date. 422 when the path date is malformed. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The router lives in `app_server/routers/rates.py` and is included by `create_app()`. It uses the\n  request-scoped database dependency from the web foundation.\n- The path date must match `YYYY-MM-DD` and be a real calendar date; otherwise respond 422 through\n  FastAPI request validation.\n- The effective date and rates come from `services.effective_date.effective_publication`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-exact ===\nIntent: A request for a publication date returns that date as both effective and requested, base EUR, and exactly that date's rates as stored text, excluding other dates.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\ntarget = \"1800-06-10\"\nother = \"1800-06-09\"\ntarget_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"1.0298\", \"ZZB\": \"160.2300\", \"ZZC\": \"0.84453\"}\nother_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"5\", \"ZZD\": \"6\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in target_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=target, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    for cur, rate in other_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=other, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(f\"/rates/{target}\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"date\"] == target\n    assert body[\"requested\"] == target\n    assert body[\"base\"] == \"EUR\"\n    assert body[\"rates\"] == target_rates\n=== END AC rates-by-date-exact ===\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-fallback ===\nIntent: A non-publication date resolves to the latest earlier publication while echoing the requested date unchanged.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\npublished = \"1800-06-15\"\nrequested = \"1800-06-17\"\nrates_in = {\"ZZA\": \"2.5000\", \"ZZB\": \"10\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in rates_in.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=published, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(f\"/rates/{requested}\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"date\"] == published\n    assert body[\"requested\"] == requested\n    assert body[\"rates\"] == rates_in\n=== END AC rates-by-date-fallback ===\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-not-found ===\nIntent: A date before every stored publication answers 404.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=\"1800-06-20\", currency=\"ZZA\", rate=\"1\")\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\"/rates/1799-01-01\")\n    assert response.status_code == 404\n=== END AC rates-by-date-not-found ===\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-malformed ===\nIntent: A path date that is not a real YYYY-MM-DD calendar date is rejected with 422, never 404 or 500.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nclient = web_client()\nfor bad in (\"2025-13-01\", \"2025-02-30\", \"not-a-date\", \"2025-1-5\"):\n    response = client.get(f\"/rates/{bad}\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422, bad\n=== END AC rates-by-date-malformed ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Rate values are returned as the stored text in JSON strings, never as JSON numbers.\n- The route does not query the database directly; it calls the effective-date service.\n- Application startup never initializes the schema.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-RatesLatest.md ===\n# FEATURE: Rates Latest\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Serve GET /rates/latest, returning every currency's rate on the most recent publication date. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-RatesByDate.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /rates/latest |\n| Consumes | rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`GET /rates/latest` returns the rates for the latest publication without the caller knowing its date.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /rates/latest` | 200 with `{\"date\": <latest publication date>, \"requested\": \"latest\", \"base\": \"EUR\", \"rates\": {<code>: <rate>, ...}}`. 404 when the store holds no rates. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Register this route in the shared rates router in `app_server/routers/rates.py` ahead of\n  `GET /rates/{date}`, so `latest` is never parsed as a date and rejected with 422.\n- Resolve through `services.effective_date.effective_publication(db, None)`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC rates-latest-returns-latest ===\nIntent: The latest route returns the most recent publication, with requested set to the latest token, and is not shadowed by the dated route; a far-future seeded date is always the latest.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nlatest = \"9999-12-31\"\nearlier = \"9999-12-30\"\nlatest_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"1.2300\", \"ZZB\": \"45.6\"}\nearlier_rates = {\"ZZA\": \"7\", \"ZZC\": \"8\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in latest_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    for cur, rate in earlier_rates.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=earlier, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\"/rates/latest\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"date\"] == latest\n    assert body[\"requested\"] == \"latest\"\n    assert body[\"base\"] == \"EUR\"\n    assert body[\"rates\"] == latest_rates\n=== END AC rates-latest-returns-latest ===\n\n=== AC rates-latest-matches-dated ===\nIntent: The latest route and the dated route for the latest publication date return the same effective date and rates.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nlatest = \"9999-12-31\"\nrates_in = {\"ZZA\": \"0.9430\", \"ZZB\": \"11.4555\"}\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for cur, rate in rates_in.items():\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=cur, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    by_latest = client.get(\"/rates/latest\")\n    assert by_latest.status_code == 200\n    by_date = client.get(f\"/rates/{latest}\")\n    assert by_date.status_code == 200\n    a = by_latest.json()\n    b = by_date.json()\n    assert a[\"date\"] == b[\"date\"]\n    assert a[\"rates\"] == b[\"rates\"]\n    assert a[\"rates\"] == rates_in\n=== END AC rates-latest-matches-dated ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `/rates/latest` is never answered with 422 as though `latest` were a malformed date.\n- Rate values are the stored text as JSON strings.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Convert.md ===\n# FEATURE: Convert\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Serve GET /convert for an explicit date, converting an amount between two currencies with exact decimal arithmetic. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-WebFoundation.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /convert |\n| Consumes | rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nConvert an amount from one currency to another using the euro reference rates on the effective date.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /convert?from=<code>&to=<code>&amount=<amount>&date=<date>` | 200 with `{\"date\": <effective date>, \"from\": <code>, \"to\": <code>, \"amount\": <amount>, \"rate\": <rate>, \"result\": <result>}`; every value is a JSON string. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The router lives in `app_server/routers/convert.py`, included by `create_app()`. Parameters are\n  `from` (Python name via alias), `to`, `amount` and `date`.\n- The effective date and rates come from `services.effective_date.effective_publication`.\n- EUR has the implicit rate `1`; any other currency takes its stored rate on the effective date.\n- All arithmetic uses `decimal.Decimal` from the stored text. Never use floats.\n- `rate` = `rate(to) / rate(from)`, rounded half to even to six decimals, written with exactly six decimals.\n- `result` = `amount * rate(to) / rate(from)`, computed from the stored rates and never from the rounded\n  `rate`, rounded half to even to two decimals, written with exactly two decimals.\n- `amount` is echoed exactly as given, as a string, and must match `[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?`.\n- Handling of missing publications, unknown currencies and invalid parameters is specified by\n  FEATURE-ConvertErrors.md; this story implements the successful conversion.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC convert-cross-currency-fallback-date ===\nIntent: A cross conversion on a non-publication date uses the earlier effective date and returns rate and result derived from the stored rates with half-even rounding.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\npublished = \"1800-07-01\"\nrequested = \"1800-07-05\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nfrom_rate, to_rate = \"1.0298\", \"0.84453\"\namount = \"100\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=published, currency=from_cur, rate=from_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=published, currency=to_cur, rate=to_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": requested},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    rf, rt, amt = Decimal(from_rate), Decimal(to_rate), Decimal(amount)\n    rate = (rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (amt * rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    assert body[\"date\"] == published\n    assert body[\"from\"] == from_cur\n    assert body[\"to\"] == to_cur\n    assert body[\"amount\"] == amount\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-cross-currency-fallback-date ===\n\n=== AC convert-eur-legs ===\nIntent: EUR carries the implicit rate 1 as source and as target, and the amount is echoed exactly as given including its decimals.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nday = \"1800-07-10\"\ncur = \"ZZA\"\ncur_rate = \"161.77\"\namount = \"250.25\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=cur, rate=cur_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n\n    out = client.get(\"/convert\", params={\"from\": \"EUR\", \"to\": cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day})\n    assert out.status_code == 200\n    body = out.json()\n    rate = (Decimal(cur_rate) / Decimal(1)).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (Decimal(amount) * Decimal(cur_rate) / Decimal(1)).quantize(\n        Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n    )\n    assert body[\"amount\"] == amount\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n\n    back = client.get(\"/convert\", params={\"from\": cur, \"to\": \"EUR\", \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day})\n    assert back.status_code == 200\n    body = back.json()\n    rate = (Decimal(1) / Decimal(cur_rate)).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (Decimal(amount) * Decimal(1) / Decimal(cur_rate)).quantize(\n        Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN\n    )\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-eur-legs ===\n\n=== AC convert-result-from-unrounded-rate ===\nIntent: The result is computed from the stored rates, not from the six-decimal rounded rate: with a from rate 3 and a to rate 1 the exact result differs from amount times the rounded rate.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nday = \"1800-07-15\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nfrom_rate, to_rate = \"3\", \"1\"\namount = \"3000000\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=from_cur, rate=from_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=to_cur, rate=to_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    rf, rt, amt = Decimal(from_rate), Decimal(to_rate), Decimal(amount)\n    rounded_rate = (rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    exact = (amt * rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    from_rounded = (amt * rounded_rate).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    assert exact != from_rounded\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rounded_rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{exact:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-result-from-unrounded-rate ===\n\n=== AC convert-half-even-tie ===\nIntent: A result exactly halfway between two cents rounds half to even, so 0.125 at equal rates becomes the even neighbour rather than rounding up.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN, ROUND_HALF_UP\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nday = \"1800-07-20\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nsame_rate = \"2\"\namount = \"0.125\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=from_cur, rate=same_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=day, currency=to_cur, rate=same_rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount, \"date\": day},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    exact = Decimal(amount) * Decimal(same_rate) / Decimal(same_rate)\n    even = exact.quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    up = exact.quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)\n    assert even != up\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{even:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-half-even-tie ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never use floating point for rates, amounts or results; use `decimal` on the stored text.\n- `result` is never derived from the rounded `rate`.\n- `amount` is echoed exactly as received, not normalized.\n- The route does not query the database directly; it calls the effective-date service.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ConvertLatest.md ===\n# FEATURE: Convert Latest\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Make the date parameter of GET /convert optional, defaulting to the latest publication. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-Convert.md, FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md |\n| Provides | GET /convert latest default |\n| Consumes | GET /convert, rates.effective_publication |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nA caller who omits `date` gets a conversion at the most recent published rates.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `GET /convert?from=<code>&to=<code>&amount=<amount>` | As `GET /convert`, using the latest publication date; the response `date` is that publication date. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- In `app_server/routers/convert.py`, `date` becomes optional. When absent, call\n  `services.effective_date.effective_publication(db, None)`; when present, behave exactly as before.\n- Rate, result, rounding and echo rules are unchanged from FEATURE-Convert.md.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is reached through `rates.effective_publication` in FEATURE-EffectiveDate.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC convert-latest-default-date ===\nIntent: Omitting date converts at the latest publication and reports that date; a far-future seeded date is always the latest, and the values derive from its stored rates with half-even rounding.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_EVEN\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nlatest = \"9999-12-31\"\nearlier = \"9999-12-30\"\nfrom_cur, to_cur = \"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"\nfrom_rate, to_rate = \"0.83608\", \"160.99\"\namount = \"1000\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=from_cur, rate=from_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=to_cur, rate=to_rate)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=earlier, currency=from_cur, rate=\"1\")\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=earlier, currency=to_cur, rate=\"1\")\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur, \"amount\": amount},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n\n    rf, rt, amt = Decimal(from_rate), Decimal(to_rate), Decimal(amount)\n    rate = (rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    result = (amt * rt / rf).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n    assert body[\"date\"] == latest\n    assert body[\"amount\"] == amount\n    assert body[\"rate\"] == f\"{rate:.6f}\"\n    assert body[\"result\"] == f\"{result:.2f}\"\n=== END AC convert-latest-default-date ===\n\n=== AC convert-latest-explicit-date-still-honoured ===\nIntent: currency=from_cur, rate=old_from)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=old_day, currency=to_cur, rate=old_to)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=from_cur, rate=\"1\")\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=latest, currency=to_cur, rate=\"1\")\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\n        \"/convert\",\n        params={\"from\": from_cur, \"to\": to_cur,
